).

(6)

When
B_{i}(b)\leq E_{i}(b)=F_{i}(b)>\beta_{\tau(i)}
and
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}-1
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b)=E_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)},\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b)-s_{i}=F_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}(b),\\
&\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}+b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\notin\mathcal{B}.\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
4
), (
7b
), and (
7c
).

Let
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathcal{B}
be such that
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})\neq 0
. Then, we have either
b^{\prime}=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}
or
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime\prime}=b^{\prime\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime\prime}_{2}=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}
. By our assumption, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}-1,\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b)\leq\varphi_{i},\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b)+1\leq\varphi_{i}+1,\end{split}

and hence,

(15)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})=\varepsilon-1=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=(E_{i}(b^{\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

Also, we compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})=\varphi_{i}-1,\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})=B_{i}(b)\leq\varphi_{i},\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})\leq E_{i}(b)=\varphi_{i}.\end{split}

This shows that

(16)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})=E_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime}_{2})=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime}_{2})=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}-1=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})-s_{i}=(E_{i}(b^{\prime\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime}_{2})\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime\prime}).\end{split}

Identities (
15
) and (
16
) confirm Definition
3.3.2
(
7d
).

Now, we compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1,\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1<\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}=\varphi_{\tau(i)},\\
&\widetilde{F}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{2}=b_{2}\neq 0,\\
&\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{F}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{2})=\varphi_{i}=\varphi_{i}(b^{\prime}_{2})+1.\end{split}

This implies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b,\end{split}

which shows that

(17)

\displaystyle(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=(b,\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}).

Also, noting that
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}-1
is equivalent to
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)},\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1<\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime})=\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}=\varphi_{\tau(i)},\\
&\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime\prime}_{2}=b_{2}\neq 0,\\
&\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime\prime}_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime\prime}_{2}).\end{split}

This implies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b^{\prime\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime\prime}_{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b,\end{split}

which shows that

(18)

\displaystyle(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime\prime})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=(b,\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime\prime}).

Identities (
17
) and (
18
) confirm Definition
3.3.2
(
3
).

(7)

When
F_{i}(b)>B_{i}(b),E_{i}(b)
, and
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b)=F_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\varphi_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i},\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}=F_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}(b).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7b
) and (
7c
).

Let
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathcal{B}
be such that
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})\neq 0
. Then, we have
b^{\prime}=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b
. By our assumption, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}-1,\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b)<\varphi_{i},\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}\leq E_{i}(b)+1\leq\varphi_{i}.\end{split}

We claim that
E_{i}(b^{\prime})<\varphi_{i}
. Otherwise, by the last inequality, we must have
E_{i}(b)+1=F_{i}(b)
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1
. However, in this case, it holds that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}
, which contradicts our assumption that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=b^{\prime}
. Thus, our claim follows. Then, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=F_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2}),\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=F_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7d
).

Now, we compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\leq\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1<\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1,\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1\leq B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1<\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}.\end{split}

This implies that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}),B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})\leq E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. We claim that
\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}
. Otherwise, we have either
\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}
or
\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}+b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{2})
. In each case, by consideration above, we obtain
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b^{\prime}
, which is a contradiction because
b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}=b
. Thus, it follows that

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}=b.

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

(8)

When
F_{i}(b)\leq B_{i}(b)>E_{i}(b)
,
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2}
, and
B_{i}(b)=\beta_{\tau(i)}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b)=B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)},\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b)-s_{i}=B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}(b).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7b
) and (
7c
).

Let
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathcal{B}
be such that
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})\neq 0
. Then, we have
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1},b^{\prime}_{1})\neq 0
and
b^{\prime}_{2}=b_{2}
. By our assumption, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=F_{i}(b)\leq B_{i}(b),\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)+s_{i}=\begin{cases}B_{i}(b)+1&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
B_{i}(b)+2&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})\neq B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
\end{cases}\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b)+3\leq B_{i}(b)+2.\end{split}

Let us consider the case when
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
and
E_{i}(b^{\prime})<B_{i}(b)+2
. By Proposition
3.3.12
, we have
b^{\prime}_{1}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}
,
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})=\beta_{i}-2
, and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)},\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=B_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7d
). Also, We compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}<\beta_{i},\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}+1=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})+1=\beta_{i}-1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)-s_{i}+1\leq\beta_{i}-2.\end{split}

This implies that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})\leq B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})>E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. Now, as in the previous case, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}=b.

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

Next, let us consider the case when
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
and
E_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b)+2
. In this case, we have
b^{\prime}_{1}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}
,
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})=\beta_{i}-2
, and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}-1=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=(E_{i}(b^{\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7d
). We compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}<\beta_{i},\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}+1=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})+1=\beta_{i}-1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)-s_{i}+1\leq\beta_{i}-2.\end{split}

This implies that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})\leq B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})>E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. Hence, we have

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}=b.

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

Finally, let us consider the case when
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})\neq B_{i}(b^{\prime})
. By Proposition
3.3.12
, we have
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{i}-1
,
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}+1
, and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}-1=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=(B_{i}(b^{\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7d
). We compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}<\beta_{i},\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}+1=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})+1=\beta_{i}-1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)-s_{i}+1\leq\beta_{i}-2.\end{split}

This implies that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})\leq B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})>E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. Hence, we have

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2},

which implies that
\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=b
if
b^{\prime}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b
, and that

(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})=(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1},b^{\prime}_{1})=(b_{1},\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{1})=(b,\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}).

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

(9)

When
F_{i}(b)\leq B_{i}(b)>E_{i}(b)
,
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2}
, and
B_{i}(b)\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b)=B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)},\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b)-s_{i}=\begin{cases}(B_{i}(b)-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}&\text{ if }F_{i}(b)<B_{i}(b),\\
B_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}&\text{ if }F_{i}(b)=B_{i}(b).\end{cases}\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7b
).

Let
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathcal{B}
be such that
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})\neq 0
. Then, we have
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1},b^{\prime}_{1})\neq 0
and
b^{\prime}_{2}=b_{2}
. Since
B_{i}(b)\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}
, it follows from Proposition
3.3.12
that
b^{\prime}_{1}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}
,
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})\neq B_{i}(b^{\prime})
, and
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})=\beta_{i}-1
. By our assumption, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=F_{i}(b)\leq B_{i}(b),\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)+s_{i}=B_{i}(b)+2,\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b)+3\leq B_{i}(b)+2.\end{split}

This implies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=(B_{i}(b^{\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7c
) and (
7d
) (note that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2}=b^{\prime}\in\mathcal{B}
).

We compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}<\beta_{i},\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{1})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})-s_{i}+1=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}_{1})=\beta_{i}-1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}+(\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-3)-s_{i}+1\leq\beta_{i}-2.\end{split}

This implies that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})\leq B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})>E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. Hence, we have

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}=b.

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

(10)

When
F_{i}(b),B_{i}(b)\leq E_{i}(b)
and
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b)=E_{i}(b)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}.\end{split}

Let
b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes b^{\prime}_{2}\in\mathcal{B}
be such that
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b,b^{\prime})\neq 0
. Then, we have
b^{\prime}=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b
. By our assumption, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\leq\varphi_{i}\leq E_{i}(b),\\
&B_{i}(b^{\prime})=B_{i}(b)\leq E_{i}(b),\\
&E_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b)+1.\end{split}

This implies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})=E_{i}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})=\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})=\beta_{i}(b)-1,\\
&\beta_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b^{\prime})-s_{i}=(E_{i}(b^{\prime})-1)+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}_{2})\neq\beta_{i}(b^{\prime}).\end{split}

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
7c
) and (
7d
).

We compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1,\\
&B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\beta_{\tau(i)}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1\leq\beta_{i}+1\leq\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1,\\
&E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=\varphi_{i}(b^{\prime}_{2})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1\leq\varphi_{i}+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}-s_{i}+1\leq\varphi_{\tau(i)}+1.\end{split}

We claim that
F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})>B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime}),E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. Assume contrary that
E_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. In this case, we must have
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}
and
F_{i}(b)=E_{i}(b)
. This implies that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}
, which contradicts our assumption that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=b^{\prime}
. Next, assume contrary that
B_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})=F_{\tau(i)}(b^{\prime})
. In this case, we must have
B_{i}(b)=\beta_{\tau(i)}
,
B_{i}(b)=E_{i}(b)
. If
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})<E_{i}(b)
, then it follows that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}
, which causes a contradiction again. Hence, we obtain
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=E_{i}(b)
. Since
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\leq\varphi_{i}\leq E_{i}(b)
, we must have
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}
and
F_{i}(b)=E_{i}(b)
. This implies that
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}
, which is a contradiction. Thus, our claim follows. Consequently, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{\tau(i)}b^{\prime}=b^{\prime}_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b^{\prime}_{2}=b.

This confirms Definition
3.3.2
(
3
) and (
4
).

Now, we have exhausted all the cases. Hence, the proof completes.

7.2.
Proof of Proposition
5.1.3

Let
b_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{i}
,
i=1,2,3
, and set
b:=b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})
,
b^{\prime}:=(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}
. It suffices to show that
\beta_{i}(b)=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})
and
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}
for all
i\in I
.

For a later use, we note the following:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\max(\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\varphi_{i}(b_{2})),\\
&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\begin{cases}b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})>\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})\leq\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\end{cases}\\
&\varepsilon_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\max(\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}),\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}))-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})\\
&\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\begin{cases}\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})>\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}),\\
b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})\leq\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}).\end{cases}\end{split}

7.2.1.
When
a_{i,\tau(i)}=2

Setting

\displaystyle\begin{split}&A_{1}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})+\delta_{\overline{\beta_{i}(b_{1})+1},\overline{\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})}},\\
&A_{2}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{2})+\delta_{\overline{\beta_{i}(b_{1})+1},\overline{\varphi_{i}(b_{2})}},\\
&A_{3}:=\beta_{i}(b_{1}),\\
&A_{4}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&A_{5}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\end{split}

we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b)=\max(A_{1},A_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b)=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b)=\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\\
&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{1}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b^{\prime})=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad E_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&F_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{2},\quad B_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{4}.\end{split}

Therefore, we compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\beta_{i}(b)&=\max(\max(A_{1},A_{2}),A_{3},\max(A_{4},A_{5}))-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})\\
&=\max(A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},A_{5})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{3}),\\
\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})&=\max(A_{1}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}))-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{3})\\
&=\max(A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},A_{5})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{3}).\end{split}

This implies that
\beta_{i}(b)=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})
. Also, we compute as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b&=\begin{cases}b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\max(A_{1},A_{2})>A_{3},\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\max(A_{1},A_{2})\leq A_{3}>\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\\
b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\max(A_{1},A_{2}),A_{3}\leq\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}>A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},A_{5},\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}\leq A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4},A_{5},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4},A_{5},\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}\leq A_{4}>A_{5},\\
b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}\leq A_{5},\end{cases}\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}>\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}),A_{5},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}\leq\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})>A_{5},\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1},\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})\leq A_{5},\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}>A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},A_{5},\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}\leq A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4},A_{5},\\
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4},A_{5},\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}\leq A_{4}>A_{5},\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}\leq A_{5}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we obtain
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}
.

7.2.2.
When
a_{i,\tau(i)}=0

Setting

\displaystyle\begin{split}&A_{1}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&A_{2}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&A_{3}:=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1}),\\
&A_{4}:=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1}),\\
&A_{5}:=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1}),\end{split}

we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b)=\max(A_{1},A_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b)=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b)=\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\\
&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{1}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b^{\prime})=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad E_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&F_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{2},\quad B_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{4}.\end{split}

Therefore, we can compute as before to obtain
\beta_{i}(b)=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})
and
\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}
.

7.2.3.
When
a_{i,\tau(i)}=-1

Setting

\displaystyle\begin{split}&A_{1}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&A_{2}:=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&A_{3}:=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1})+s_{i},\\
&A_{4}:=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1})+s_{i},\\
&A_{5}:=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})-\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1})+s_{i},\end{split}

we see that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b)=\max(A_{1},A_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b)=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b)=\max(A_{4},A_{5}),\\
&F_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{1}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad B_{i}(b^{\prime})=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\quad E_{i}(b^{\prime})=A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&F_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{2},\quad B_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{3},\quad E_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{4}.\end{split}

Hence, we can compute as before to obtain
\beta_{i}(b)=\beta_{i}(b^{\prime})
.

Note also that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})&=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})+\operatorname{wt}^{\imath}_{i}(b_{1})-s_{i}-\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}),\\
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})&=\max(A_{4}-1,\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),A_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})\\
&=\begin{cases}\max(A_{4}-1,A_{3},A_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3},\\
\max(A_{4}-1,A_{3}-1,A_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\neq A_{3}.\end{cases}\end{split}

(1)

When
A_{1}>A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},A_{5}
. In this case, we have

\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3},

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=\max(A_{4},A_{5})+1,\\
&\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\max(\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}),\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}))
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\max(A_{4},A_{5})
, we have
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1
if and only if
A_{4}\leq A_{5}
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+1
. Therefore, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}+1\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Note that
A_{1}=A_{5}+1
implies
A_{4}\leq A_{1}-1=A_{5}
.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}+1\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(2)

When
A_{1}\leq A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4},A_{5}
and
A_{4}>A_{5}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\end{split}

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}+1,\\
&\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})
, we have
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1
if and only if
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1
. Therefore, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}+1\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

On the other hand, since
A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4}
, we have

\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{2}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),

and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

However, since
A_{2}>A_{4}>A_{5}
, it never happens that
A_{2}=A_{5}+1
. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}+1\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(3)

When
A_{1}\leq A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4},A_{5}
and
A_{4}\leq A_{5}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\end{split}

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})
, we always have
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1
. Therefore, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5}+1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

On the other hand, since
A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4}
, we have

\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{2}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),

and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Since
A_{2}>A_{3},A_{4}
, we have
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\in\mathcal{B}_{1}\otimes\mathcal{B}_{2}
if and only if
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}
. In this case, we always have
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2
. Otherwise, we have either
\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}=0
or
A_{2}=A_{4}+1
and
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1
. Noting that
A_{2}=A_{4}+1
implies
A_{2}=A_{5}+1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5}+1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(4)

When
A_{1},A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4},A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3}
. In this case, we have

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{3}=\max(A_{4},A_{5})+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2,\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise},\end{cases}

On the other hand, since
A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3}
, we have

\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=A_{3}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{3}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise},\end{cases}

Since
A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4}
, we have
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\in\mathcal{B}_{1}\otimes\mathcal{B}_{2}
if and only if
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2}\in\mathcal{B}_{1}\otimes\mathcal{B}_{2}
. In this case, the equality
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2
is equivalent to that
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2
and
A_{3}+1\geq A_{4}+3
since we have

(19)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=F_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}),\\
&B_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})+(\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})-\beta_{i}(b_{1}))+3,\\
&E_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=E_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})+3.\end{split}

Now, consider the case when
A_{4}>A_{5}
. In this case, it never happens that
A_{3}=A_{5}+1
. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{3}=A_{4}+1\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2,\\
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Next, consider the case when
A_{4}\leq A_{5}
. Noting that
A_{3}=A_{4}+1
implies
A_{3}=A_{5}+1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{3}=A_{5}+1\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2,\\
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

In each case, we have

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(5)

When
A_{1},A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4},A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\neq A_{3}
. In this case, it never happens that
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2
, and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3}))&\text{ if }\max(A_{1},A_{2})=A_{3},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}))&\text{ if }A_{2}<A_{1}=A_{3},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3}))&\text{ if }A_{1}\leq A_{2}=A_{3},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

On the other hand, since
A_{2}\leq A_{3}>A_{4}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\neq A_{3}
, we have

\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=\max(A_{3}-1,A_{2})-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})=\begin{cases}B_{i}(b^{\prime})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{3},\\
B_{i}(b^{\prime})-1&\text{ if }A_{2}<A_{3},\end{cases}

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{3}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{3}>A_{2},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\neq A_{3}
, it never happens that
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1})=\beta_{i}(b_{1})-2
. Hence, by identities (
19
), it never happens that
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2
.

Now, consider the case when
A_{1}>A_{2}
. In this case, we have
A_{2}<A_{3}
, and hence

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{3},\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}<A_{3}\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{3},\\
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}<A_{3}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Next, consider the case when
A_{1}\leq A_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{3},\\
(\widetilde{B}_{i}b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}<A_{3}.\end{cases}\end{split}

In each case, we have

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(6)

When
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}\leq A_{4}>A_{5}
and
A_{1}>A_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})-1=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})-1=A_{1}-1,\\
&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=A_{4},\end{split}

and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3}=A_{4},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}))&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}>\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3}=A_{4},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}))&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}>A_{3},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{1}=A_{4}=A_{3}\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}\end{cases}\end{split}

On the other hand, we have

\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=\begin{cases}B_{i}(b^{\prime})-1&\text{ if }A_{4}>A_{3},A_{2},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{4}\leq A_{3}>A_{2}\and\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\neq A_{3},\\
B_{i}(b^{\prime})&\text{ otherwise},\end{cases}

and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{4}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}>A_{3},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{1}=A_{4}=A_{3}\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Since
A_{2},A_{3}\leq A_{4}
, we have
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\in\mathcal{B}_{1}\otimes\mathcal{B}_{2}
if and only if
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\neq 0
. In this case it never happens that
\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2
since we have.

\displaystyle\begin{split}&F_{i}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\leq A_{2},\\
&B_{i}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=A_{3},\\
&E_{i}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=A_{4}+1.\end{split}

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}>A_{3},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{1}=A_{4}=A_{3}\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}>A_{3},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{1}=A_{4}=A_{3}\neq\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{3}=A_{4}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(7)

When
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}\leq A_{4}>A_{5}
and
A_{1}\leq A_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\begin{cases}\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})-1&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2})-1,\end{cases}\\
&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\begin{cases}\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2})+1,\\
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}).\end{cases}\end{split}

and hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&\text{ or }A_{3}=A_{4}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})<A_{4},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}>\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
+b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3}))&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2})-1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{4}=A_{5}+1,\\
&\and\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2,\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{4}\and\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\neq B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

As in the previous case, it never happens that
\beta_{i}(\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2}))=\beta_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})-2
. Also, since
A_{1}\leq A_{2}
, the condition that
A_{1}=A_{4}
implies
A_{2}=A_{4}
, which, in turn, shows that
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
.
Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\widetilde{B}_{i}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes b_{3}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}),\\
&\text{ or }A_{3}=A_{4}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})<A_{4},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{4}>\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
+(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2})\otimes b_{3})&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2})-1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{2})\otimes b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(8)

When
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}\leq A_{5}
and
A_{1}>A_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})=\begin{cases}\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3}),\\
\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})-1&\text{ if }\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})-1,\end{cases}\\
&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\begin{cases}\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+1,\\
\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})&\text{ if }\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3}),\end{cases}\end{split}

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3}),\\
&\text{ or }A_{3}=A_{5}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})+\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})<A_{5},\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}>\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
+b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3}))&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})-1,\\
b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})&\text{ otherwise},\end{cases}

On the other hand, we have

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3}),\\
&\text{ or }A_{5}=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}),\ \beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5},\ \beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})-1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since we have
A_{2}<A_{1}
, we have
A_{5}=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
if and only if
A_{3}=A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3}
. Also, we have
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})
if and only if either
A_{3}<A_{5}
, or
A_{3}=A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\neq B_{i}(b^{\prime})
. This condition is equivalent to that either
A_{3}<A_{5}
, or
A_{3}=A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})<A_{3}
. This is, in turn, equivalent to
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})<A_{5}
. Therefore, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3}),\\
&\text{ or }A_{3}=A_{5}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}((b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}>\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}),\\
+(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{3})&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3})-1,\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

(9)

When
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}\leq A_{5}
and
A_{1}\leq A_{2}
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2})=\varphi_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3}),\\
&\widetilde{F}_{i}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3},\\
&\varphi_{\tau(i)}(\widetilde{F}_{i}b_{2}\otimes b_{3})=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{3})+1=\varphi_{\tau(i)}(b_{2}\otimes b_{3})+1,\end{split}

and hence,

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{3}=A_{5}=\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})\and A_{2}<A_{5},\\
b_{1}\otimes(b_{2}\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

On the other hand, since
A_{1}=A_{5}
implies
A_{2}=A_{5}
, and hence,
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
, we have

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{1}=A_{5}\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})=\varphi_{i}(b_{3}),\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{5}=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}),\ \beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
&\and\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{i}b_{3})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2}),\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

Now, consider the case when
A_{1}=A_{5}
. In this case, we automatically have
A_{2}=A_{5}
and
\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime})
. Then, regardless of the value
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})
, we obtain

\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}=\widetilde{B}_{i}b.

Next, consider the case when
A_{1}<A_{5}
. In this case, we always have
\varphi_{i}(\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3})\leq A_{1}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})<A_{5}-\operatorname{wt}_{i}(b_{2})
. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{5}=\max(A_{2},A_{3},A_{4})\and\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})=B_{i}(b^{\prime}),\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}\end{cases}\\
&=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ if }A_{2}=A_{5},\text{ or }\\
&\ A_{2}<A_{3}=A_{5}\and\beta_{\tau(i)}(b_{1})=A_{3},\\
(b_{1}\otimes b_{2})\otimes\widetilde{E}_{\tau(i)}b_{3}&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}\end{split}

Thus, we conclude

\widetilde{B}_{i}b=\widetilde{B}_{i}b^{\prime}.

Now, we have exhausted all the cases. Hence, the proof completes.
